after: from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import List, Optional
from .models import Phase, Card, Deck, CardType

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards, player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.life = 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates
        self.aging_normal_deck = Deck()
        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0

    def get_target_value(self) -> int:
        """Calculates the current target value based on the phase and active hazard."""
        if not self.active_hazard:
            return 0
        if self.phase == Phase.GREEN:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_green
        elif self.phase == Phase.YELLOW:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_yellow
        elif self.phase == Phase.RED:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_red
        else: # PIRATES
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_red

    def get_combat_total(self) -> int:
        """Calculates current Robinson combat value (ignoring special edge cases like 'Highest = 0' for now)."""
        return sum(c.robinson_value for c in self.cards_in_play)

    def _shuffle_robinson_deck(self):
        """Shuffle discard pile into draw pile, adding an aging card."""
        aging_card = self.aging_normal_deck.draw()
        if not aging_card:
            aging_card = self.aging_hard_deck.draw()
            
        if aging_card:
            self.robinson_discard.add(aging_card)
            
        self.robinson_deck.cards.extend(self.robinson_discard.cards)
        self.robinson_discard.cards.clear()
        self.robinson_deck.shuffle()

    def step_draw_hazards(self):
        """Action: Draw 2 hazards from the deck to choose from."""
        if self.state != GameState.START_TURN:
            raise ValueError("Can only draw hazards at the start of a turn.")

        if self.hazard_deck.is_empty():
            if self.phase == Phase.GREEN:
                self.phase = Phase.YELLOW
            elif self.phase == Phase.YELLOW:
                self.phase = Phase.RED
            elif self.phase == Phase.RED:
                self.phase = Phase.PIRATES
                return
            
            self.hazard_deck.cards.extend(self.hazard_discard.cards)
            self.hazard_discard.cards.clear()
            self.hazard_deck.shuffle()

        c1 = self.hazard_deck.draw()
        c2 = self.hazard_deck.draw()

        if c1: self.hazards_drawn_for_choice.append(c1)
        if c2: self.hazards_drawn_for_choice.append(c2)

        self.state = GameState.CHOOSE_HAZARD

    def step_choose_hazard(self, index: int):
        """Action: Pick one of the drawn hazards. Discard the other."""
        if self.state != GameState.CHOOSE_HAZARD:
            raise ValueError("Not in choose hazard state.")

        self.active_hazard = self.hazards_drawn_for_choice.pop(index)

        # The unchosen one goes to the hazard discard pile
        for c in self.hazards_drawn_for_choice:
            self.hazard_discard.add(c)
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice.clear()

        self.free_draws_remaining = self.active_hazard.hazard_free_draws
        self.state = GameState.FIGHTING

    def step_draw_combat_card(self):
        """Action: Draw 1 card from the Robinson deck to fight the hazard."""
        if self.state != GameState.FIGHTING:
            raise ValueError("Can only draw combat cards while FIGHTING.")
            
        if self.free_draws_remaining > 0:
            self.free_draws_remaining -= 1
        else:
            self.life -= 1
            if self.life < 0:
                self.state = GameState.GAME_OVER_LOSS
                return

        if self.robinson_deck.is_empty():
            self._shuffle_robinson_deck()
            
        card = self.robinson_deck.draw()
        if card:
            self.cards_in_play.append(card)

    def step_conclude_combat(self):
        """Action: The player decides to stop drawing and conclude the fight."""
        if self.state != GameState.FIGHTING:
            raise ValueError("Not in FIGHTING state.")
            
        combat_total = self.get_combat_total()
        target = self.get_target_value()
        
        if combat_total >= target:
            # Win!
            if self.active_hazard:
                self.robinson_discard.add(self.active_hazard)
                self.active_hazard = None
                
            for c in self.cards_in_play:
                self.robinson_discard.add(c)
            self.cards_in_play.clear()
            
            self.state = GameState.START_TURN
        else:
            # Loss.
            loss_amount = target - combat_total
            self.life -= loss_amount
            if self.life < 0:
                self.state = GameState.GAME_OVER_LOSS
                return
                
            self.destroy_points = loss_amount
            self.state = GameState.RESOLVING_LOSS

    def step_destroy_card(self, index: int):
        """Action: During loss resolution, destroy a card in play using destroy points."""
        if self.state != GameState.RESOLVING_LOSS:
            raise ValueError("Not in RESOLVING_LOSS state.")
            
        card = self.cards_in_play[index]
        cost = 2 if card.card_type in (CardType.AGING_NORMAL, CardType.AGING_HARD) else 1
        
        if self.destroy_points >= cost:
            self.destroy_points -= cost
            self.cards_in_play.pop(index)

    def step_finish_resolution(self):
        """Action: Finish the loss resolution step, discarding remaining cards."""
        if self.state != GameState.RESOLVING_LOSS:
            raise ValueError("Not in RESOLVING_LOSS state.")
            
        if self.active_hazard:
            self.hazard_discard.add(self.active_hazard)
            self.active_hazard = None
            
        for c in self.cards_in_play:
            self.robinson_discard.add(c)
        self.cards_in_play.clear()
        
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

```
    def __repr__(self):
        return (f"<FridayEngine Phase:{self.phase.name} Life:{self.life} "
                f"State:{self.state.name} InPlay:{len(self.cards_in_play)}>")
```

    # We have a strong card in deck
    c1 = Card("Strong", CardType.STARTING)
    c1.robinson_value = 2
    engine.robinson_deck.add(c1)
    
    engine.step_draw_hazards()
    engine.step_choose_hazard(0)
    
    assert engine.state == GameState.FIGHTING
    assert engine.free_draws_remaining == 1
    
    engine.step_draw_combat_card()
    assert engine.free_draws_remaining == 0
    assert engine.life == 20
    assert len(engine.cards_in_play) == 1
    assert engine.get_combat_total() == 2
    
    # We meet the target of 2, so we can conclude and win
    engine.step_conclude_combat()
    assert engine.state == GameState.START_TURN
    # Hazard and played card go to Robinson discard
    assert len(engine.robinson_discard) == 2
    assert h1 in engine.robinson_discard.cards
    assert c1 in engine.robinson_discard.cards
    assert engine.active_hazard is None

def test_combat_loop_loss_and_destroy():
    engine = FridayEngine()
    
    # Hazard requires 2 power
    h1 = Card("H1", CardType.HAZARD)
    h1.hazard_free_draws = 1
    h1.hazard_green = 2
    engine.hazard_deck.add(h1)
    
    # We have a weak card in deck
    c1 = Card("Weak", CardType.STARTING)
    c1.robinson_value = 0
    engine.robinson_deck.add(c1)
    
    engine.step_draw_hazards()
    engine.step_choose_hazard(0)
    
    engine.step_draw_combat_card()
    assert engine.get_combat_total() == 0
    
    # Yielding combat (Target 2, Total 0 -> Lose 2 life)
    engine.step_conclude_combat()
    assert engine.state == GameState.RESOLVING_LOSS
    assert engine.life == 18
    assert engine.destroy_points == 2
    
    # We can spend 1 destroy point to trash our weak card (index 0)
    engine.step_destroy_card(0)
    assert len(engine.cards_in_play) == 0
    assert engine.destroy_points == 1
    
    # Finish resolution
    engine.step_finish_resolution()
    assert engine.state == GameState.START_TURN
    assert engine.active_hazard is None
    # Hazard went to hazard discard, weak card is destroyed (gone forever)
    assert len(engine.hazard_discard) == 1
    assert len(engine.robinson_discard) == 0

def test_deck_exhaustion_aging_card():
    engine = FridayEngine()
    engine.state = GameState.FIGHTING
    engine.free_draws_remaining = 1
    
    # Robinson deck is empty. Discard has 1 card.
    engine.robinson_deck.cards.clear()
    c1 = Card("Weak", CardType.STARTING)
    engine.robinson_discard.add(c1)
    
    # Setup Aging Deck
    a1 = Card("Aging1", CardType.AGING_NORMAL)
    engine.aging_normal_deck.add(a1)
    
    # Drawing a card should trigger shuffle and inject the aging card
    engine.step_draw_combat_card()
    
    # We drew 1 card (it's in play)
    assert len(engine.cards_in_play) == 1
    # 1 card should be left in deck
    assert len(engine.robinson_deck) == 1
    # Discard should be empty
    assert len(engine.robinson_discard) == 0
    # Both cards should exist across play/deck
    all_cards = engine.cards_in_play + engine.robinson_deck.cards
    assert a1 in all_cards
    assert c1 in all_cards
    assert len(engine.aging_normal_deck) == 0

```
from enum import Enum, auto
import random
from typing import List, Optional

class Phase(Enum):
    GREEN = 0
    YELLOW = 1
    RED = 2
    PIRATES = 3

class CardType(Enum):
    STARTING = auto()       # The weak Robinson cards you start with
    HAZARD = auto()         # Cards drawn from the hazard deck
    AGING_NORMAL = auto()   # White aging cards
    AGING_HARD = auto()     # Black aging cards
    PIRATE = auto()         # Boss cards

class Ability(Enum):
    NONE = auto()
    PLUS_1_LIFE = auto()
    PLUS_2_LIFE = auto()
    PLUS_1_CARD = auto()
    PLUS_2_CARD = auto()
    DESTROY = auto()
    DOUBLE = auto()
    COPY = auto()
    PHASE_DOWN = auto()     # Treat hazard as 1 phase lower
    SORT_3 = auto()
    EXCHANGE_1 = auto()
    EXCHANGE_2 = auto()
    STEP_MINUS_1 = auto()   # Needs special below-the-line handling
    STOP = auto()           # Let's you end the fight immediately without paying life (wait, standard rules let you stop anytime, but we will refine)
    HIGHEST_ZERO = auto()   # Special aging penalty

class Card:
    def __init__(self, card_id: str, card_type: CardType):
        self.card_id = card_id
        self.card_type = card_type

        # Hazard properties (if it's a hazard or pirate)
        self.hazard_free_draws: int = 0
        self.hazard_green: int = 0
        self.hazard_yellow: int = 0
        self.hazard_red: int = 0

        # Robinson properties (if it's a played card or starting card)
        self.robinson_value: int = 0
        self.robinson_ability: Ability = Ability.NONE

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"<Card {self.card_id} [{self.card_type.name}] val:{self.robinson_value}>"


class Deck:
    def __init__(self, cards: Optional[List[Card]] = None):
        self.cards: List[Card] = cards if cards is not None else []

    def add(self, card: Card):
        self.cards.append(card)

    def draw(self) -> Optional[Card]:
        if not self.cards:
            return None
        return self.cards.pop(0)

    def shuffle(self):
        random.shuffle(self.cards)

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.cards)

    def is_empty(self) -> bool:
        return len(self.cards) == 0
```

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards, player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.life = 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates

        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()

        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0

        """Calculates the current target value based on the phase and active hazard."""
        if not self.active_hazard:
            return 0
        if self.phase == Phase.GREEN:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_green
        elif self.phase == Phase.YELLOW:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_yellow
        elif self.phase == Phase.RED:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_red
        else: # PIRATES
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_red

    def get_combat_total(self) -> int:
        """Calculates current Robinson combat value (ignoring special edge cases like 'Highest = 0' for now)."""
        return sum(c.robinson_value for c in self.cards_in_play)

after: from .models import Phase, Card, Deck, CardType, Ability

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards, player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.life = 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates
        self.aging_normal_deck = Deck()
        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0
        
        # State tracking for USE_ABILITY
        self.active_ability_source_index = -1
        self.active_ability = Ability.NONE

    def get_target_value(self) -> int:
        """Calculates the current target value based on the phase and active hazard."""
        if not self.active_hazard:
            return 0
            
        effective_phase_value = self.phase.value + self.phase_modifier
        if effective_phase_value < 0:
            effective_phase_value = 0
            
        if effective_phase_value == 0:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_green
        elif effective_phase_value == 1:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_yellow
        else:
            return self.active_hazard.hazard_red

    def get_combat_total(self) -> int:
        """Calculates current Robinson combat value."""
        total = 0
        for c in self.cards_in_play:
            val = c.robinson_value
            if c.is_doubled:
                val *= 2
            total += val
        return total

        if self.state != GameState.FIGHTING:
            raise ValueError("Can only use abilities while FIGHTING.")
            
        card = self.cards_in_play[index]
        if card.ability_used:
            raise ValueError("Ability already used.")
            
        ability = card.robinson_ability
        if ability == Ability.NONE:
            raise ValueError("Card has no ability.")

        # Resolve immediate abilities
        if ability == Ability.PLUS_1_LIFE:
            self.life += 1
            card.ability_used = True
        elif ability == Ability.PLUS_2_LIFE:
            self.life += 2
            card.ability_used = True
        elif ability == Ability.PLUS_1_CARD:
            self.free_draws_remaining += 1
            card.ability_used = True
        elif ability == Ability.PLUS_2_CARD:
            self.free_draws_remaining += 2
            card.ability_used = True
        elif ability == Ability.PHASE_DOWN:
            self.phase_modifier -= 1
            card.ability_used = True
        elif ability in (Ability.DESTROY, Ability.DOUBLE):
            # Enters targeted ability mode
            self.state = GameState.USE_ABILITY
            self.active_ability_source_index = index
            self.active_ability = ability
        else:
            # TODO: Implement other complex abilities (SORT_3, EXCHANGE, COPY)
            raise NotImplementedError(f"Ability {ability.name} not fully implemented yet.")

    def step_resolve_ability_target(self, target_index: int):
        """Action: Player provides the target for an ability (like Destroy or Double)."""
        if self.state != GameState.USE_ABILITY:
            raise ValueError("Not waiting for ability target.")
            
        target_card = self.cards_in_play[target_index]
        source_card = self.cards_in_play[self.active_ability_source_index]
        
        if self.active_ability == Ability.DESTROY:
            # The destroyed card is removed from play and game
            self.cards_in_play.pop(target_index)
            # If we destroyed the source card itself, that's valid, but we need to handle index shifting
            # Let's just mark source as used (if it wasn't the one destroyed)
            if source_card in self.cards_in_play:
                source_card.ability_used = True
                
        elif self.active_ability == Ability.DOUBLE:
            target_card.is_doubled = True
            source_card.ability_used = True
            
        self.active_ability = Ability.NONE
        self.active_ability_source_index = -1
        
        if self.state == GameState.USE_ABILITY: # Just to be safe if a transition happened
            self.state = GameState.FIGHTING

    def step_conclude_combat(self):

content: import pytest
from friday.engine.models import Card, CardType, Ability
from friday.engine.game import FridayEngine, GameState

def setup_engine_with_active_combat():
    engine = FridayEngine()
    engine.state = GameState.FIGHTING
    
    h1 = Card("H1", CardType.HAZARD)
    h1.hazard_green = 5
    engine.active_hazard = h1
    
    return engine

def test_ability_plus_life():
    engine = setup_engine_with_active_combat()
    engine.life = 10
    
    c1 = Card("LifeCard", CardType.STARTING)
    c1.robinson_ability = Ability.PLUS_2_LIFE
    engine.cards_in_play.append(c1)
    
    engine.step_use_ability(0)
    
    assert engine.life == 12
    assert c1.ability_used is True
    
    # Can't use twice
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        engine.step_use_ability(0)

def test_ability_phase_down():
    engine = setup_engine_with_active_combat()
    # Phase is GREEN, hazard value is 5
    assert engine.get_target_value() == 5
    
    c1 = Card("PhaseCard", CardType.STARTING)
    c1.robinson_ability = Ability.PHASE_DOWN
    engine.cards_in_play.append(c1)
    
    engine.step_use_ability(0)
    
    # Phase goes down by 1. Since GREEN is 0, effective phase is 0 (can't go negative)
    # Actually wait, if we are in YELLOW (1), it goes to GREEN (0). Let's test that.
    engine.phase_modifier = 0 # reset
    c1.ability_used = False
    
    from friday.engine.models import Phase
    engine.phase = Phase.YELLOW
    engine.active_hazard.hazard_yellow = 8
    
    assert engine.get_target_value() == 8
    engine.step_use_ability(0)
    assert engine.get_target_value() == 5 # Falls back to Green value

def test_ability_double():
    engine = setup_engine_with_active_combat()
    
    c1 = Card("DoubleCard", CardType.STARTING)
    c1.robinson_ability = Ability.DOUBLE
    
    c2 = Card("TargetCard", CardType.STARTING)
    c2.robinson_value = 3
    
    engine.cards_in_play.extend([c1, c2])
    
    assert engine.get_combat_total() == 3
    
    engine.step_use_ability(0)
    assert engine.state == GameState.USE_ABILITY
    
    engine.step_resolve_ability_target(1) # Target c2
    assert engine.state == GameState.FIGHTING
    
    assert engine.get_combat_total() == 6
    assert c1.ability_used is True
    assert c2.is_doubled is True

## Agent (2026-08-29 15:52:40)

I have implemented the **Card Abilities** engine!

To support this, I made a few architectural improvements to the state machine:
1.  **State Tracking**: Cards now track their own `ability_used: bool` and `is_doubled: bool` state while in play. When combat concludes, a `reset_state()` method wipes this clean before the card hits the discard pile.
2.  **`step_use_ability(index)`**: Added a new action endpoint. If a player activates a simple immediate ability (like `+2 Life`, `+1 Card`, or `Phase -1`), it applies the effect and marks the ability as used instantly.
3.  **Targeted Abilities (`USE_ABILITY` state)**: If a player uses an ability like `Destroy` or `Double`, the game machine drops out of `FIGHTING` and enters `USE_ABILITY`, waiting for the player to call `step_resolve_ability_target(target_index)` to apply the effect to another card.

I wrote a new test suite file (`tests/test_abilities.py`) testing these mechanics:
*   `test_ability_plus_life`: Validates instantaneous resolution and ensures abilities cannot be fired twice.
*   `test_ability_phase_down`: Validates the `Phase -1` ability properly alters the `target_value` dynamically (e.g., overriding a Yellow hazard to evaluate using its Green score).
*   `test_ability_double`: Validates the targeted ability workflow. Activating "Double", entering the waiting state, selecting a 3-value card, and validating the `combat_total` correctly yields 6.

All 8 tests in the suite pass. The backend is humming along perfectly!

Should we tackle the **Pirate Boss mechanics** next to finish off the core backend logic?

## Agent's Thoughts (2026-08-29 15:53:57)

**Defining Pirate Boss Mechanics**

Okay, I'm defining the core mechanics for the Pirate Boss in *Friday*. I'm focusing on how the Pirate Phase works. Specifically, the number of pirates to fight (is it 1 or 2?). I'm also considering the implications of choosing pirates, if that is the case.


**Refining Pirate Abilities**

Okay, I've clarified the pirate combat order: you fight all surviving pirates, which are usually two drawn at the game's start. Now, I'm working through the specific abilities. The "Each Hazard +2" has been updated to relate to the correct rules of the game. I am currently generalizing pirate abilities with the Enum `PirateAbility`, and exploring the nuances of abilities like "Half all cards (rounded up)."


**Enumerating Pirate Abilities**

I've been working on defining the specific pirate abilities. I've realized that the initial "Each Hazard +2" concept needs refinement because pirates *are* hazards. The update focuses on defining the abilities as an enum. I've decided on the `PirateAbility` enum and implemented the abilities as `NONE`, `PLUS_1_PER_CARD`, `PLUS_2_PER_AGING`, and `HALF_COMBAT` within `models.py`.


**Implementing Pirate Combat**

I've been integrating the pirate combat logic into `game.py`. I've added an `active_pirates` list to `FridayEngine` for handling the initial pirate draw in the `PIRATES` phase. I'm modifying `get_target_value()` to incorporate `PirateAbility` modifiers like `PLUS_1_PER_CARD` and `PLUS_2_PER_AGING`. Finally, I've implemented the `HALF_COMBAT` ability, using integer division for clarity and consistency with Friday's rules.

        """Action: Pick one of the drawn hazards (or pirates). Discard the other."""
        if self.state != GameState.CHOOSE_HAZARD:
            raise ValueError("Not in choose hazard state.")

        self.active_hazard = self.hazards_drawn_for_choice.pop(index)

        if self.phase == Phase.PIRATES:
            # For pirates, the one we picked is removed from active_pirates
            if self.active_hazard in self.active_pirates:
                self.active_pirates.remove(self.active_hazard)
            # The others go back to active_pirates (they stay drawn/face up for next time)
            self.hazards_drawn_for_choice.clear()
        else:
            # The unchosen hazard goes to the hazard discard pile
            for c in self.hazards_drawn_for_choice:
                self.hazard_discard.add(c)
            self.hazards_drawn_for_choice.clear()

        self.free_draws_remaining = self.active_hazard.hazard_free_draws
        self.state = GameState.FIGHTING

content: import pytest
from friday.engine.models import Card, CardType, PirateAbility, Phase
from friday.engine.game import FridayEngine, GameState

def test_pirate_phase_transition():
    engine = FridayEngine()
    engine.phase = Phase.RED
    
    # Empty the hazard deck to trigger transition
    engine.hazard_deck.cards.clear()
    engine.hazard_discard.cards.clear()
    
    # Setup Pirates
    p1 = Card("P1", CardType.PIRATE)
    p2 = Card("P2", CardType.PIRATE)
    engine.active_pirates = [p1, p2]
    
    # Draw should fail over to PIRATES phase and present pirates
    engine.step_draw_hazards()
    
    assert engine.phase == Phase.PIRATES
    assert engine.state == GameState.CHOOSE_HAZARD
    assert len(engine.hazards_drawn_for_choice) == 2
    assert p1 in engine.hazards_drawn_for_choice
    
def test_pirate_half_combat():
    engine = FridayEngine()
    engine.phase = Phase.PIRATES
    engine.state = GameState.FIGHTING
    
    p = Card("Pirate Half", CardType.PIRATE)
    p.hazard_red = 10
    p.pirate_ability = PirateAbility.HALF_COMBAT
    engine.active_hazard = p
    
    c1 = Card("Value3", CardType.STARTING)
    c1.robinson_value = 3
    engine.cards_in_play.append(c1)
    
    # Combat total should be 3 // 2 = 1
    assert engine.get_combat_total() == 1

def test_pirate_plus_2_per_aging():
    engine = FridayEngine()
    engine.phase = Phase.PIRATES
    
    p = Card("Pirate Aging", CardType.PIRATE)
    p.hazard_red = 10
    p.pirate_ability = PirateAbility.PLUS_2_PER_AGING
    engine.active_hazard = p
    
    # Base target is 10
    assert engine.get_target_value() == 10
    
    # Add an aging card to play
    a1 = Card("Aging", CardType.AGING_NORMAL)
    engine.cards_in_play.append(a1)
    
    # Target should now be 12
    assert engine.get_target_value() == 12

def test_pirate_plus_1_per_card():
    engine = FridayEngine()
    engine.phase = Phase.PIRATES
    
    p = Card("Pirate Cards", CardType.PIRATE)
    p.hazard_red = 10
    p.pirate_ability = PirateAbility.PLUS_1_PER_CARD
    engine.active_hazard = p
    
    assert engine.get_target_value() == 10
    
    c1 = Card("Card1", CardType.STARTING)
    c2 = Card("Card2", CardType.STARTING)
    engine.cards_in_play.extend([c1, c2])
    
    # 2 cards in play -> Target = 12
    assert engine.get_target_value() == 12

## Agent (2026-08-29 15:56:26)

I have successfully implemented the Pirate Boss mechanics!

Here is how I integrated them into the engine:
1.  **Phase Transitions**: When the `RED` hazard deck is empty, the engine automatically transitions to `Phase.PIRATES`. Instead of drawing from the empty hazard deck, `step_draw_hazards()` now presents you with the cards sitting in `engine.active_pirates`. 
2.  **Fighting Pirates**: When you pick one, the unchosen pirate isn't discarded. It sits back waiting for you to fight it next, exactly as the rules dictate.
3.  **Pirate Abilities (`PirateAbility`)**: I added logic for the three nastiest Pirate mechanics:
    *   `HALF_COMBAT`: When calculating your combat total, it halves your total points (rounded down) dynamically.
    *   `PLUS_2_PER_AGING`: The target value you have to beat goes up by 2 dynamically every time you place an Aging card into play.
    *   `PLUS_1_PER_CARD`: The target value goes up by 1 for *every single card* you play, making deck-spamming dangerous.

I created `tests/test_pirates.py` with 4 isolated tests covering the phase transition and validating the math for all three unique Pirate abilities. The entire test suite (now 12 tests) passes cleanly!

The core backend Game Engine for *Friday* is fundamentally complete and mathematically sound.

What is the next step? We have two options:
1. Create a script to parse the JPEG assets and extract the card metadata to start making actual playable decks.
2. Build the Pygame CE scaffolding (window, event loop, renderer) so we can start seeing things on screen.

```
--

<p>However, life on the island is energy-sapping for Robinson so he is forced to always add aging cards to his fighting cards pile. These cards are all cumbersome, so you&#39;d better remember to keep them in mind when planning your next steps.</p>

<p>It is up to you. Use Robinson&#39;s life points cleverly and choose the correct hazards to keep Robinson healthy and strong for the final fights against both pirates.<span id="ezoic-pub-ad-placeholder-129" class="ezoic-adpicker-ad"></span><span data-ez-ph-id="129"></span></p>

<p>The game offers several difficulty levels. Firstly, we explain the easiest game to you - Level 1. As soon as you win this level, you can try three additional levels and try to play against the next challenge. Do you finally beat the game in Level 4?</p>

<img src="gfx/components.jpg" class="floatr"/>

<br/><span id="ezoic-pub-ad-placeholder-709" data-method="ai-placeholders" class="ezoic-autoinsert-ad"></span><span data-ez-ph-id="709"></span>
<h2>Components</h2>
<ul>
<li>3 step cards of green, yellow and red</li>
<li>59 fighting cards:</li>
<li>10 pirate cards</li>
<li>22 life points</li>
<li>3 Storage Boards</li>
</ul>

<br class="break"/>

--
<br/><span id="ezoic-pub-ad-placeholder-710" data-method="ai-placeholders" class="ezoic-autoinsert-ad"></span><span data-ez-ph-id="710"></span>
<h2>Setup</h2>

<style>.tokenlabel { background-color: #000;}</style>

<p>Your first game starts in Level 1:</p>

<p><s class="tokenlabel">1</s> Sort the step cards and place the pile in front of you. The green card should be visible on top of the pile, below are the yellow and red cards. The game starts with the green step.</p>

<p><s class="tokenlabel">2</s> Remove the aging card &#34;Very Stupid&#34; from the game. Sort the remaining 10 aging cards in two piles ( 7x normal <s class="tokenlabel">A</s> and 3x difficult <s class="tokenlabel">B</s> ), shuffle both piles and place them as a single face-down stack on the aging storage board. Place the normal aging cards <s class="tokenlabel">A</s> on top of the stack. You will need these aging cards later during the game.</p>

<p><s class="tokenlabel">3</s> Shuffle the 18 starting cards and place the face-down stack on the Robinson storage board in front of yourself. These are your first fighting cards.</p>

<p><s class="tokenlabel">4</s> Shuffle the 30 hazard cards and place the face-down stack on the hazard storage board.<span id="ezoic-pub-ad-placeholder-116" class="ezoic-adpicker-ad"></span><span data-ez-ph-id="116"></span></p>

<p><s class="tokenlabel">5</s> Shuffle the Pirate cards, draw two random pirates and place them face-up as your final opponents. Place the remaining Pirate cards back in the game box.</p>

<p><s class="tokenlabel">6</s> Take 20 life points for Robinson and place them next to your Robinson stack. Place 2 more life points as the reserve next to the play area.</p>

<p><s class="tokenlabel">7</s> Set aside some space next to the Robinson and hazard stacks for the appropriate discard piles.</p>

--

<br/>

<blockquote class="white">

<h3>Difficulty Levels</h3>

<p>The game offers you different difficulty levels. You can try to fight the growing challenges, until you finally win the game in the highest level! You need to make the following changes in Preparations:</p>

<p><b>Level 1 (as written in these rules):</b> The aging card <i>Very Stupid</i> is removed from the game. Start with 18 Robinson starting cards and 20 life points plus 2 more life points in the reserve.</p>

<p><b>Level 2:</b> Like Level 1. In addition you draw a face-down aging card and shuffle it together with the 18 Robinson starting cards (do not look at the aging card!).</p>

<p><b>Level 3:</b> Like Level 2. Before performing all the steps above, shuffle the Very Stupid card together with the other aging cards.</p>

<p><b>Level 4:</b> Like Level 3. Start with only 18 life points plus 2 life points in the reserve. This is the real game!</p>

</blockquote>

<blockquote class="white">

<h4>Scoring</h4>

<p>After each game you can calculate your victory points. Will you get better with more experience?</p>

<ol>
<li><p>Take all your fighting cards (together with your Robinson discard pile) and add all the fighting values. All aging cards have a value of -5 regardless of their real fighting points for this purpose.</p></li>

<li><p>Add 15 points for each defeated pirate card (undefeated pirates are ignored).</p></li>

<li><p>Add 5 points for each remaining life point you still have in Robinson&#39;s storage (you only have them if you win the game).</p></li>

<li><p>Subtract 3 points for each unbeaten hazard card still in the hazard discard pile.</p></li>
</ol>

<p>When calculating the scores you can compare your different games and successes in each Level. The higher the level, the more difficult it will be for you to get a high score.</p>

</blockquote>
```

from typing import List, Optional
from .models import Phase, Card, Deck, CardType, Ability, PirateAbility

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.life = 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates
        self.aging_normal_deck = Deck()
        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()
        
        self.active_pirates: List[Card] = []

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0

after: from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import List, Optional
from .models import Phase, Card, Deck, CardType, Ability, PirateAbility, GameLevel

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self, level: GameLevel = GameLevel.LEVEL_1):
        self.level = level
        self.life = 20 if level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_1, GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3) else 18
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates
        self.aging_normal_deck = Deck()
        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()
        
        self.active_pirates: List[Card] = []
        self.defeated_pirates: List[Card] = []

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0
        
        # State tracking for USE_ABILITY
        self.active_ability_source_index = -1
        self.active_ability = Ability.NONE
        
    def setup_game(self, starting_cards: List[Card], hazards: List[Card], pirates: List[Card], 
                   normal_aging: List[Card], hard_aging: List[Card]):
        """Initializes the deck contents based on the selected difficulty level."""
        # 1. Starting Robinson Deck
        self.robinson_deck.cards = starting_cards.copy()
        
        # 2. Aging Decks
        self.aging_normal_deck.cards = normal_aging.copy()
        self.aging_hard_deck.cards = hard_aging.copy()
        
        # Handle Level 3 & 4 (Include 'Very Stupid' in hard aging)
        # Assuming the caller provided all cards, but if "Very Stupid" is a specific card, 
        # it should only be in `hard_aging` if level >= 3. (We will handle this by filtering if necessary, 
        # but for engine purity we'll assume the caller passes the right pool. 
        # However, Level 2-4 inject an aging card immediately!)
        
        if self.level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3, GameLevel.LEVEL_4):
            self.aging_normal_deck.shuffle()
            initial_aging_penalty = self.aging_normal_deck.draw()
            if initial_aging_penalty:
                self.robinson_deck.add(initial_aging_penalty)
                
        self.robinson_deck.shuffle()
        
        # 3. Hazards and Pirates
        self.hazard_deck.cards = hazards.copy()
        self.hazard_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.pirate_deck.cards = pirates.copy()
        self.pirate_deck.shuffle()
        
        # Draw 2 active pirates for the endgame
        p1 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        p2 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        if p1: self.active_pirates.append(p1)
        if p2: self.active_pirates.append(p2)

    def calculate_score(self) -> int:
        """Calculates endgame score based on the official rules (Page 15)."""
        score = 0
        
        # 1. Add fighting values of all Robinson cards. Aging cards are strictly -5.
        all_robinson_cards = self.robinson_deck.cards + self.robinson_discard.cards + self.cards_in_play
        for c in all_robinson_cards:
            if c.card_type in (CardType.AGING_NORMAL, CardType.AGING_HARD):
                score -= 5
            else:
                score += c.robinson_value
                
        # 2. Add 15 points for each defeated pirate card
        score += (len(self.defeated_pirates) * 15)
        
        # 3. Add 5 points for each remaining life point (Even if lost, but usually only positive if alive? Rules say "remaining life")
        if self.life > 0:
            score += (self.life * 5)
            
        # 4. Subtract 3 points for each unbeaten hazard card
        # This includes hazards in the deck, discard, and active_hazard if unresolved.
        unbeaten_hazards = len(self.hazard_deck) + len(self.hazard_discard) + len(self.hazards_drawn_for_choice)
        if self.active_hazard and self.active_hazard.card_type != CardType.PIRATE:
            unbeaten_hazards += 1
            
        score -= (unbeaten_hazards * 3)
        
        return score


from typing import List, Optional
from .models import Phase, Card, Deck, CardType, Ability, PirateAbility, GameLevel

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self, level: GameLevel = GameLevel.LEVEL_1):
        self.level = level
        # Level 1, 2, 3 start with 20 life (plus 2 reserve which we bundle in GUI, but logically let's say 20 is the main pool). Wait, rules say "20 plus 2 reserve" = 22 total.
        # So we'll give them 22. Level 4 gets 18+2 = 20 total.
        self.life = 22 if level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_1, GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3) else 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates
        self.aging_normal_deck = Deck()
        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()
        
        self.active_pirates: List[Card] = []
        self.defeated_pirates: List[Card] = []

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0
        
        # State tracking for USE_ABILITY
        self.active_ability_source_index = -1
        self.active_ability = Ability.NONE

    def setup_game(self, starting_cards: List[Card], hazards: List[Card], pirates: List[Card], 
                   normal_aging: List[Card], hard_aging: List[Card]):
        """Initializes the deck contents based on the selected difficulty level."""
        self.robinson_deck.cards = starting_cards.copy()
        
        self.aging_normal_deck.cards = normal_aging.copy()
        self.aging_hard_deck.cards = hard_aging.copy()
        
        if self.level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3, GameLevel.LEVEL_4):
            self.aging_normal_deck.shuffle()
            initial_aging_penalty = self.aging_normal_deck.draw()
            if initial_aging_penalty:
                self.robinson_deck.add(initial_aging_penalty)
                
        self.robinson_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.hazard_deck.cards = hazards.copy()
        self.hazard_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.pirate_deck.cards = pirates.copy()
        self.pirate_deck.shuffle()
        
        p1 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        p2 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        if p1: self.active_pirates.append(p1)
        if p2: self.active_pirates.append(p2)

    def calculate_score(self) -> int:
        """Calculates endgame score based on the official rules (Page 15)."""
        score = 0
        
        # 1. Add fighting values of all Robinson cards. Aging cards are strictly -5.
        all_robinson_cards = self.robinson_deck.cards + self.robinson_discard.cards + self.cards_in_play
        for c in all_robinson_cards:
            if c.card_type in (CardType.AGING_NORMAL, CardType.AGING_HARD):
                score -= 5
            else:
                score += c.robinson_value
                
        # 2. Add 15 points for each defeated pirate card
        score += (len(self.defeated_pirates) * 15)
        
        # 3. Add 5 points for each remaining life point
        if self.life > 0:
            score += (self.life * 5)
            
        # 4. Subtract 3 points for each unbeaten hazard card
        unbeaten_hazards = len(self.hazard_deck) + len(self.hazard_discard) + len(self.hazards_drawn_for_choice)
        if self.active_hazard and self.active_hazard.card_type != CardType.PIRATE:
            unbeaten_hazards += 1
            
        score -= (unbeaten_hazards * 3)
        
        return score

        self.robinson_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.hazard_deck.cards = hazards.copy()
        self.hazard_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.pirate_deck.cards = pirates.copy()
        self.pirate_deck.shuffle()
        
        p1 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        p2 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        if p1: self.active_pirates.append(p1)
        if p2: self.active_pirates.append(p2)

    def calculate_score(self) -> int:
        """Calculates endgame score based on the official rules (Page 15)."""
        score = 0
        
        # 1. Add fighting values of all Robinson cards. Aging cards are strictly -5.
        all_robinson_cards = self.robinson_deck.cards + self.robinson_discard.cards + self.cards_in_play
        for c in all_robinson_cards:
            if c.card_type in (CardType.AGING_NORMAL, CardType.AGING_HARD):
                score -= 5
            else:
                score += c.robinson_value
                
        # 2. Add 15 points for each defeated pirate card
        score += (len(self.defeated_pirates) * 15)
        
        # 3. Add 5 points for each remaining life point
        if self.life > 0:
            score += (self.life * 5)
            
        # 4. Subtract 3 points for each unbeaten hazard card
        unbeaten_hazards = len(self.hazard_deck) + len(self.hazard_discard) + len(self.hazards_drawn_for_choice)
        if self.active_hazard and self.active_hazard.card_type != CardType.PIRATE:
            unbeaten_hazards += 1
            
        score -= (unbeaten_hazards * 3)
        
        return score

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """

        self.life = 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates

        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()
        
        self.active_pirates: List[Card] = []

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0
        
        # State tracking for USE_ABILITY
        self.active_ability_source_index = -1
        self.active_ability = Ability.NONE

after: from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import List, Optional
from .models import Phase, Card, Deck, CardType, Ability, PirateAbility, GameLevel

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self, level: GameLevel = GameLevel.LEVEL_1):
        self.level = level
        # Level 1, 2, 3 start with 20 life (plus 2 reserve which we bundle in GUI, but logically let's say 20 is the main pool). Wait, rules say "20 plus 2 reserve" = 22 total.
        # So we'll give them 22. Level 4 gets 18+2 = 20 total.
        self.life = 22 if level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_1, GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3) else 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates
        self.aging_normal_deck = Deck()
        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()
        
        self.active_pirates: List[Card] = []
        self.defeated_pirates: List[Card] = []

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0
        
        # State tracking for USE_ABILITY
        self.active_ability_source_index = -1
        self.active_ability = Ability.NONE

    def setup_game(self, starting_cards: List[Card], hazards: List[Card], pirates: List[Card], 
                   normal_aging: List[Card], hard_aging: List[Card]):
        """Initializes the deck contents based on the selected difficulty level."""
        self.robinson_deck.cards = starting_cards.copy()
        
        self.aging_normal_deck.cards = normal_aging.copy()
        self.aging_hard_deck.cards = hard_aging.copy()
        
        if self.level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3, GameLevel.LEVEL_4):
            self.aging_normal_deck.shuffle()
            initial_aging_penalty = self.aging_normal_deck.draw()
            if initial_aging_penalty:
                self.robinson_deck.add(initial_aging_penalty)
                
        self.robinson_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.hazard_deck.cards = hazards.copy()
        self.hazard_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.pirate_deck.cards = pirates.copy()
        self.pirate_deck.shuffle()
        
        p1 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        p2 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        if p1: self.active_pirates.append(p1)
        if p2: self.active_pirates.append(p2)

    def calculate_score(self) -> int:
        """Calculates endgame score based on the official rules (Page 15)."""
        score = 0
        
        # 1. Add fighting values of all Robinson cards. Aging cards are strictly -5.
        all_robinson_cards = self.robinson_deck.cards + self.robinson_discard.cards + self.cards_in_play
        for c in all_robinson_cards:
            if c.card_type in (CardType.AGING_NORMAL, CardType.AGING_HARD):
                score -= 5
            else:
                score += c.robinson_value
                
        # 2. Add 15 points for each defeated pirate card
        score += (len(self.defeated_pirates) * 15)
        
        # 3. Add 5 points for each remaining life point
        if self.life > 0:
            score += (self.life * 5)
            
        # 4. Subtract 3 points for each unbeaten hazard card
        unbeaten_hazards = len(self.hazard_deck) + len(self.hazard_discard) + len(self.hazards_drawn_for_choice)
        if self.active_hazard and self.active_hazard.card_type != CardType.PIRATE:
            unbeaten_hazards += 1
            
        score -= (unbeaten_hazards * 3)
        
        return score


    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards

    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()

    """
    def __init__(self, level: GameLevel = GameLevel.LEVEL_1):

        # So we'll give them 22. Level 4 gets 18+2 = 20 total.
        self.life = 22 if level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_1, GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3) else 20

        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()

        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()

        self.defeated_pirates: List[Card] = []

        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0

        self.robinson_deck.cards = starting_cards.copy()

        p2 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        if p1: self.active_pirates.append(p1)

        """Calculates endgame score based on the official rules (Page 15)."""
        score = 0
        
        # 1. Add fighting values of all Robinson cards. Aging cards are strictly -5.
        all_robinson_cards = self.robinson_deck.cards + self.robinson_discard.cards + self.cards_in_play
        for c in all_robinson_cards:
            if c.card_type in (CardType.AGING_NORMAL, CardType.AGING_HARD):
                score -= 5
            else:
                score += c.robinson_value
                
        # 2. Add 15 points for each defeated pirate card
        score += (len(self.defeated_pirates) * 15)
        
        # 3. Add 5 points for each remaining life point
        if self.life > 0:
            score += (self.life * 5)
            
        # 4. Subtract 3 points for each unbeaten hazard card
        unbeaten_hazards = len(self.hazard_deck) + len(self.hazard_discard) + len(self.hazards_drawn_for_choice)
        if self.active_hazard and self.active_hazard.card_type != CardType.PIRATE:
            unbeaten_hazards += 1
            
        score -= (unbeaten_hazards * 3)
        
        return score

after: from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import List, Optional
from .models import Phase, Card, Deck, CardType, Ability, PirateAbility, GameLevel

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self, level: GameLevel = GameLevel.LEVEL_1):
        self.level = level
        # Level 1, 2, 3 start with 20 life (plus 2 reserve which we bundle in GUI, but logically let's say 20 is the main pool). Wait, rules say "20 plus 2 reserve" = 22 total.
        # So we'll give them 22. Level 4 gets 18+2 = 20 total.
        self.life = 22 if level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_1, GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3) else 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates
        self.aging_normal_deck = Deck()
        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()
        
        self.active_pirates: List[Card] = []
        self.defeated_pirates: List[Card] = []

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0
        
        # State tracking for USE_ABILITY
        self.active_ability_source_index = -1
        self.active_ability = Ability.NONE

    def setup_game(self, starting_cards: List[Card], hazards: List[Card], pirates: List[Card], 
                   normal_aging: List[Card], hard_aging: List[Card]):
        """Initializes the deck contents based on the selected difficulty level."""
        self.robinson_deck.cards = starting_cards.copy()
        
        self.aging_normal_deck.cards = normal_aging.copy()
        self.aging_hard_deck.cards = hard_aging.copy()
        
        if self.level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3, GameLevel.LEVEL_4):
            self.aging_normal_deck.shuffle()
            initial_aging_penalty = self.aging_normal_deck.draw()
            if initial_aging_penalty:
                self.robinson_deck.add(initial_aging_penalty)
                
        self.robinson_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.hazard_deck.cards = hazards.copy()
        self.hazard_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.pirate_deck.cards = pirates.copy()
        self.pirate_deck.shuffle()
        
        p1 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        p2 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        if p1: self.active_pirates.append(p1)
        if p2: self.active_pirates.append(p2)

    def calculate_score(self) -> int:
        """Calculates endgame score based on the official rules (Page 15)."""
        score = 0
        
        # 1. Add fighting values of all Robinson cards. Aging cards are strictly -5.
        all_robinson_cards = self.robinson_deck.cards + self.robinson_discard.cards + self.cards_in_play
        for c in all_robinson_cards:
            if c.card_type in (CardType.AGING_NORMAL, CardType.AGING_HARD):
                score -= 5
            else:
                score += c.robinson_value
                
        # 2. Add 15 points for each defeated pirate card
        score += (len(self.defeated_pirates) * 15)
        
        # 3. Add 5 points for each remaining life point
        if self.life > 0:
            score += (self.life * 5)
            
        # 4. Subtract 3 points for each unbeaten hazard card
        unbeaten_hazards = len(self.hazard_deck) + len(self.hazard_discard) + len(self.hazards_drawn_for_choice)
        if self.active_hazard and self.active_hazard.card_type != CardType.PIRATE:
            unbeaten_hazards += 1
            
        score -= (unbeaten_hazards * 3)
        
        return score

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.

    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()

    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.

```
from enum import Enum, auto
import random
from typing import List, Optional

class Phase(Enum):
    GREEN = 0
    YELLOW = 1
    RED = 2
    PIRATES = 3

class CardType(Enum):
    STARTING = auto()       # The weak Robinson cards you start with
    HAZARD = auto()         # Cards drawn from the hazard deck
    AGING_NORMAL = auto()   # White aging cards
    AGING_HARD = auto()     # Black aging cards
    PIRATE = auto()         # Boss cards

class Ability(Enum):
    NONE = auto()
    PLUS_1_LIFE = auto()
    PLUS_2_LIFE = auto()
    PLUS_1_CARD = auto()
    PLUS_2_CARD = auto()
    DESTROY = auto()
    DOUBLE = auto()
    COPY = auto()
    PHASE_DOWN = auto()     # Treat hazard as 1 phase lower
    SORT_3 = auto()
    EXCHANGE_1 = auto()
    EXCHANGE_2 = auto()
    STEP_MINUS_1 = auto()   # Needs special below-the-line handling
    STOP = auto()           # Let's you end the fight immediately without paying life (wait, standard rules let you stop anytime, but we will refine)
    HIGHEST_ZERO = auto()   # Special aging penalty

class PirateAbility(Enum):
    NONE = auto()
    PLUS_1_PER_CARD = auto()    # Target increases by 1 for every Robinson card in play
    PLUS_2_PER_AGING = auto()   # Target increases by 2 for every Aging card in play
    HALF_COMBAT = auto()        # Robinson combat total is halved

class GameLevel(Enum):
    LEVEL_1 = 1
    LEVEL_2 = 2
    LEVEL_3 = 3
    LEVEL_4 = 4

class Card:
    def __init__(self, card_id: str, card_type: CardType):
        self.card_id = card_id
        self.card_type = card_type

        # Hazard properties (if it's a hazard or pirate)
        self.hazard_free_draws: int = 0
        self.hazard_green: int = 0
        self.hazard_yellow: int = 0
        self.hazard_red: int = 0
        self.pirate_ability: PirateAbility = PirateAbility.NONE

        # Robinson properties (if it's a played card or starting card)
        self.robinson_value: int = 0
        self.robinson_ability: Ability = Ability.NONE

        # In-play state tracking
        self.ability_used: bool = False
        self.is_doubled: bool = False

    def reset_state(self):
        self.ability_used = False
        self.is_doubled = False

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"<Card {self.card_id} [{self.card_type.name}] val:{self.robinson_value}>"


class Deck:
    def __init__(self, cards: Optional[List[Card]] = None):
        self.cards: List[Card] = cards if cards is not None else []

    def add(self, card: Card):
        self.cards.append(card)

    def draw(self) -> Optional[Card]:
        if not self.cards:
            return None
        return self.cards.pop(0)

    def shuffle(self):
        random.shuffle(self.cards)

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.cards)

    def is_empty(self) -> bool:
        return len(self.cards) == 0
```

from typing import List, Optional
from .models import Phase, Card, Deck, CardType, Ability, PirateAbility, GameLevel

class GameState(Enum):
    START_TURN = auto()
    CHOOSE_HAZARD = auto()    # Drawn 2 hazards (or active pirates), player must pick 1
    FIGHTING = auto()         # Actively playing cards / drawing free cards
    RESOLVING_LOSS = auto()   # Player chose to yield (or couldn't beat it). Must pick cards to destroy.
    USE_ABILITY = auto()      # Waiting on player to select target for an ability (like Sort 3 or Copy)
    GAME_OVER_WIN = auto()
    GAME_OVER_LOSS = auto()

class FridayEngine:
    """
    The headless backend engine. Manages the complete state of a game of Friday.
    """
    def __init__(self, level: GameLevel = GameLevel.LEVEL_1):
        self.level = level
        self.life = 22 if level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_1, GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3) else 20
        self.phase = Phase.GREEN
        self.state = GameState.START_TURN

        # Decks
        self.robinson_deck = Deck()
        self.robinson_discard = Deck()
        self.hazard_deck = Deck()
        self.hazard_discard = Deck()

        # Aging and Pirates
        self.aging_normal_deck = Deck()
        self.aging_hard_deck = Deck()
        self.pirate_deck = Deck()
        
        self.active_pirates: List[Card] = []
        self.defeated_pirates: List[Card] = []

        # Current active zone
        self.active_hazard: Optional[Card] = None
        self.hazards_drawn_for_choice: List[Card] = []
        self.cards_in_play: List[Card] = []

        self.free_draws_remaining = 0
        self.destroy_points = 0
        self.phase_modifier = 0
        
        # State tracking for USE_ABILITY
        self.active_ability_source_index = -1
        self.active_ability = Ability.NONE

    def setup_game(self, starting_cards: List[Card], hazards: List[Card], pirates: List[Card], 
                   normal_aging: List[Card], hard_aging: List[Card]):
        """Initializes the deck contents based on the selected difficulty level."""
        self.robinson_deck.cards = starting_cards.copy()
        
        self.aging_normal_deck.cards = normal_aging.copy()
        self.aging_hard_deck.cards = hard_aging.copy()
        
        if self.level in (GameLevel.LEVEL_2, GameLevel.LEVEL_3, GameLevel.LEVEL_4):
            self.aging_normal_deck.shuffle()
            initial_aging_penalty = self.aging_normal_deck.draw()
            if initial_aging_penalty:
                self.robinson_deck.add(initial_aging_penalty)
                
        self.robinson_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.hazard_deck.cards = hazards.copy()
        self.hazard_deck.shuffle()
        
        self.pirate_deck.cards = pirates.copy()
        self.pirate_deck.shuffle()
        
        p1 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        p2 = self.pirate_deck.draw()
        if p1: self.active_pirates.append(p1)
        if p2: self.active_pirates.append(p2)

    def calculate_score(self) -> int:
        """Calculates endgame score based on the official rules (Page 15)."""
        score = 0
        
        # 1. Add fighting values of all Robinson cards. Aging cards are strictly -5.
        all_robinson_cards = self.robinson_deck.cards + self.robinson_discard.cards + self.cards_in_play
        for c in all_robinson_cards:
            if c.card_type in (CardType.AGING_NORMAL, CardType.AGING_HARD):
                score -= 5
            else:
                score += c.robinson_value
                
        # 2. Add 15 points for each defeated pirate card
        score += (len(self.defeated_pirates) * 15)
        
        # 3. Add 5 points for each remaining life point
        if self.life > 0:
            score += (self.life * 5)
            
        # 4. Subtract 3 points for each unbeaten hazard card
        unbeaten_hazards = len(self.hazard_deck) + len(self.hazard_discard) + len(self.hazards_drawn_for_choice)
        if self.active_hazard and self.active_hazard.card_type != CardType.PIRATE:
            unbeaten_hazards += 1
            
        score -= (unbeaten_hazards * 3)
        
        return score

    def get_target_value(self) -> int:

content: import pytest
from friday.engine.models import Card, CardType, GameLevel
from friday.engine.game import FridayEngine

def test_scoring_system():
    engine = FridayEngine(level=GameLevel.LEVEL_1)
    
    # 1. 22 Life points * 5 = 110 points
    engine.life = 22
    
    # 2. Defeated 2 Pirates * 15 = 30 points
    p1 = Card("P1", CardType.PIRATE)
    p2 = Card("P2", CardType.PIRATE)
    engine.defeated_pirates.extend([p1, p2])
    
    # 3. Robinson Cards (Values +2, +1, and -5 for an Aging Card)
    c1 = Card("Strong", CardType.STARTING)
    c1.robinson_value = 2
    c2 = Card("Weak", CardType.STARTING)
    c2.robinson_value = 1
    a1 = Card("Aging", CardType.AGING_NORMAL)
    # Aging cards give -5 regardless of their printed value
    
    engine.robinson_deck.cards.extend([c1, c2, a1])
    
    # 4. Unbeaten Hazards (-3 each)
    h1 = Card("H1", CardType.HAZARD)
    h2 = Card("H2", CardType.HAZARD)
    engine.hazard_deck.cards.append(h1)
    engine.hazard_discard.cards.append(h2)
    
    # Calculate expected score:
    # Life: 22 * 5 = 110
    # Pirates: 2 * 15 = 30
    # Cards: 2 + 1 - 5 = -2
    # Hazards: 2 * (-3) = -6
    # Total: 110 + 30 - 2 - 6 = 132
    
    assert engine.calculate_score() == 132

def test_level_setup():
    # Test level 4 initializes with 20 life instead of 22
    engine4 = FridayEngine(level=GameLevel.LEVEL_4)
    assert engine4.life == 20
    
    # Setup injects aging card into deck for Level >= 2
    engine2 = FridayEngine(level=GameLevel.LEVEL_2)
    start_cards = [Card(f"S{i}", CardType.STARTING) for i in range(18)]
    normal_aging = [Card(f"A{i}", CardType.AGING_NORMAL) for i in range(7)]
    hard_aging = [Card(f"H{i}", CardType.AGING_HARD) for i in range(3)]
    
    engine2.setup_game(
        starting_cards=start_cards,
        hazards=[Card("Haz", CardType.HAZARD)],
        pirates=[Card("P1", CardType.PIRATE), Card("P2", CardType.PIRATE)],
        normal_aging=normal_aging,
        hard_aging=hard_aging
    )
    
    # Deck should have 18 starting + 1 aging = 19 cards
    assert len(engine2.robinson_deck) == 19
    # Aging deck should now only have 6 normal cards left
    assert len(engine2.aging_normal_deck) == 6

```
+import pytest
+from friday.engine.models import Card, CardType, GameLevel
+from friday.engine.game import FridayEngine
+
+def test_scoring_system():
+    engine = FridayEngine(level=GameLevel.LEVEL_1)
+    
+    # 1. 22 Life points * 5 = 110 points
+    engine.life = 22
+    
+    # 2. Defeated 2 Pirates * 15 = 30 points
+    p1 = Card("P1", CardType.PIRATE)
+    p2 = Card("P2", CardType.PIRATE)
+    engine.defeated_pirates.extend([p1, p2])
+    
+    # 3. Robinson Cards (Values +2, +1, and -5 for an Aging Card)
+    c1 = Card("Strong", CardType.STARTING)
+    c1.robinson_value = 2
+    c2 = Card("Weak", CardType.STARTING)
+    c2.robinson_value = 1
+    a1 = Card("Aging", CardType.AGING_NORMAL)
+    # Aging cards give -5 regardless of their printed value
+    
+    engine.robinson_deck.cards.extend([c1, c2, a1])
+    
+    # 4. Unbeaten Hazards (-3 each)
+    h1 = Card("H1", CardType.HAZARD)
+    h2 = Card("H2", CardType.HAZARD)
+    engine.hazard_deck.cards.append(h1)
+    engine.hazard_discard.cards.append(h2)
+    
+    # Calculate expected score:
+    # Life: 22 * 5 = 110
+    # Pirates: 2 * 15 = 30
+    # Cards: 2 + 1 - 5 = -2
+    # Hazards: 2 * (-3) = -6
+    # Total: 110 + 30 - 2 - 6 = 132
+    
+    assert engine.calculate_score() == 132
+
+def test_level_setup():
+    # Test level 4 initializes with 20 life instead of 22
+    engine4 = FridayEngine(level=GameLevel.LEVEL_4)
+    assert engine4.life == 20
+    
+    # Setup injects aging card into deck for Level >= 2
+    engine2 = FridayEngine(level=GameLevel.LEVEL_2)
+    start_cards = [Card(f"S{i}", CardType.STARTING) for i in range(18)]
+    normal_aging = [Card(f"A{i}", CardType.AGING_NORMAL) for i in range(7)]
+    hard_aging = [Card(f"H{i}", CardType.AGING_HARD) for i in range(3)]
+    
+    engine2.setup_game(
+        starting_cards=start_cards,
+        hazards=[Card("Haz", CardType.HAZARD)],
+        pirates=[Card("P1", CardType.PIRATE), Card("P2", CardType.PIRATE)],
+        normal_aging=normal_aging,
+        hard_aging=hard_aging
+    )
+    
+    # Deck should have 18 starting + 1 aging = 19 cards
+    assert len(engine2.robinson_deck) == 19
+    # Aging deck should now only have 6 normal cards left
+    assert len(engine2.aging_normal_deck) == 6
```

content: import sys
from friday.engine.models import Card, CardType, Phase, Ability, PirateAbility, GameLevel
from friday.engine.game import FridayEngine, GameState

def create_mock_decks():
    # Starting Deck
    starting = []
    for _ in range(8): starting.append(Card("Weak", CardType.STARTING)) # 0
    for _ in range(5): 
        c = Card("Distracted", CardType.STARTING)
        c.robinson_value = -1
        starting.append(c)
    c_gen = Card("Genius", CardType.STARTING); c_gen.robinson_value = 2; starting.append(c_gen)
    for _ in range(2):
        c = Card("Food", CardType.STARTING)
        c.robinson_ability = Ability.PLUS_1_LIFE
        starting.append(c)
    for _ in range(2):
        c = Card("Focused", CardType.STARTING)
        c.robinson_value = 1
        starting.append(c)
        
    # Hazards
    hazards = []
    for i in range(10):
        h = Card(f"Explore {i}", CardType.HAZARD)
        h.hazard_free_draws = 2
        h.hazard_green, h.hazard_yellow, h.hazard_red = 1, 3, 6
        h.robinson_value = 1
        hazards.append(h)
    for i in range(10):
        h = Card(f"Wild Animals {i}", CardType.HAZARD)
        h.hazard_free_draws = 1
        h.hazard_green, h.hazard_yellow, h.hazard_red = 4, 7, 11
        h.robinson_value = 2
        h.robinson_ability = Ability.PLUS_1_CARD
        hazards.append(h)
    for i in range(10):
        h = Card(f"Cannibals {i}", CardType.HAZARD)
        h.hazard_free_draws = 3
        h.hazard_green, h.hazard_yellow, h.hazard_red = 5, 9, 14
        h.robinson_value = 3
        h.robinson_ability = Ability.DESTROY
        hazards.append(h)
        
    # Aging
    normal_aging = []
    for i in range(7):
        a = Card(f"Scared {i}", CardType.AGING_NORMAL)
        a.robinson_value = -2
        normal_aging.append(a)
    hard_aging = []
    for i in range(3):
        a = Card(f"Very Stupid {i}", CardType.AGING_HARD)
        a.robinson_value = -4
        hard_aging.append(a)
        
    # Pirates
    pirates = []
    p1 = Card("Pirate Ship", CardType.PIRATE)
    p1.hazard_red = 15; p1.pirate_ability = PirateAbility.HALF_COMBAT
    p2 = Card("Pirate Crew", CardType.PIRATE)
    p2.hazard_red = 20; p2.pirate_ability = PirateAbility.PLUS_2_PER_AGING
    p3 = Card("Pirate Captain", CardType.PIRATE)
    p3.hazard_red = 12; p3.pirate_ability = PirateAbility.PLUS_1_PER_CARD
    pirates.extend([p1, p2, p3, Card("Pirate Raft", CardType.PIRATE)])

    return starting, hazards, pirates, normal_aging, hard_aging

def print_game_state(engine: FridayEngine):
    print("\n" + "="*50)
    print(f"PHASE: {engine.phase.name} | LIFE: {engine.life} | TARGET: {engine.get_target_value()} | COMBAT TOTAL: {engine.get_combat_total()}")
    print(f"RobDeck: {len(engine.robinson_deck)} | RobDisc: {len(engine.robinson_discard)} | HazDeck: {len(engine.hazard_deck)} | HazDisc: {len(engine.hazard_discard)}")
    
    if engine.active_hazard:
        if engine.phase == Phase.PIRATES:
            print(f"FIGHTING PIRATE: {engine.active_hazard.card_id} [Target: {engine.active_hazard.hazard_red}] (Ability: {engine.active_hazard.pirate_ability.name})")
        else:
            h = engine.active_hazard
            print(f"ACTIVE HAZARD: {h.card_id} [Target {h.hazard_green}/{h.hazard_yellow}/{h.hazard_red}] (Free draws: {engine.free_draws_remaining})")
        
    if engine.cards_in_play:
        print("\nCARDS IN PLAY:")
        for i, c in enumerate(engine.cards_in_play):
            used = "(USED)" if c.ability_used else ""
            dbl = "(DOUBLED)" if c.is_doubled else ""
            print(f"  [{i}] {c.card_id} (Val: {c.robinson_value}) [Ability: {c.robinson_ability.name}] {used} {dbl}")
    print("="*50)

def main():
    engine = FridayEngine()
    starting, hazards, pirates, normal_aging, hard_aging = create_mock_decks()
    engine.setup_game(starting, hazards, pirates, normal_aging, hard_aging)
    
    print("Welcome to FRIDAY (CLI Edition)")
    
    while True:
        if engine.state == GameState.GAME_OVER_WIN:
            print(f"\nYOU WON! Final Score: {engine.calculate_score()}")
            break
        elif engine.state == GameState.GAME_OVER_LOSS:
            print(f"\nYOU DIED. Final Score: {engine.calculate_score()}")
            break
            
        print_game_state(engine)
        
        if engine.state == GameState.START_TURN:
            input("Press Enter to draw hazards...")
            engine.step_draw_hazards()
            
        elif engine.state == GameState.CHOOSE_HAZARD:
            print("\nChoose a Hazard/Pirate:")
            for i, h in enumerate(engine.hazards_drawn_for_choice):
                if engine.phase == Phase.PIRATES:
                    print(f"  [{i}] {h.card_id} (Target: {h.hazard_red})")
                else:
                    print(f"  [{i}] {h.card_id} (Target: {h.hazard_green}/{h.hazard_yellow}/{h.hazard_red}, Free: {h.hazard_free_draws})")
            
            while True:
                choice = input("Enter 0 or 1: ").strip()
                if choice in ('0', '1'):
                    engine.step_choose_hazard(int(choice))
                    break
                    
        elif engine.state == GameState.FIGHTING:
            print("\nOptions: [d]raw card, [c]onclude combat, [u]se ability")
            choice = input("Action (d/c/u #): ").strip().lower()
            
            if choice == 'd':
                engine.step_draw_combat_card()
            elif choice == 'c':
                engine.step_conclude_combat()
            elif choice.startswith('u'):
                try:
                    idx = int(choice.split()[1])
                    engine.step_use_ability(idx)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Error using ability: {e}")
                    
        elif engine.state == GameState.RESOLVING_LOSS:
            print(f"\nYou lost. You must pay {engine.destroy_points} life (already deducted).")
            print(f"You have {engine.destroy_points} destroy points to spend on cards in play.")
            print("\nOptions: [d]estroy card #, [f]inish resolution")
            choice = input("Action (d #/f): ").strip().lower()
            
            if choice.startswith('d'):
                try:
                    idx = int(choice.split()[1])
                    engine.step_destroy_card(idx)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Error destroying card: {e}")
            elif choice == 'f':
                engine.step_finish_resolution()
                
        elif engine.state == GameState.USE_ABILITY:
            print(f"\nSelect target for {engine.active_ability.name}")
            choice = input("Target card index #: ").strip()
            try:
                engine.step_resolve_ability_target(int(choice))
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error targeting: {e}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

## Agent (2026-08-29 16:47:46)

I have created the interactive terminal game! 

I populated it with an authentic starting layout:
- 18 starting cards (mostly 0s and weak abilities, mixed with a few negatives and one 2).
- 30 hazard cards of three varied difficulty tiers.
- 10 aging cards (normal and hard).
- 4 boss pirates to draw from.

You can now play the game in your terminal directly! 

Run the following command:
```bash
cd ~/src/friday && python3 play.py
```

The game loop handles all the phases automatically. 
- At the start of a turn, it will draw 2 hazards. 
- You type `0` or `1` to choose which hazard you want to fight.
- During combat, type `d` to draw cards. When you run out of free draws, drawing costs life. 
- You can type `u 0` to use the ability of the 0th card in play.
- Type `c` to conclude combat. If your total is higher than the target, you win and add the hazard to your deck. If it's lower, you lose life but get destroy points to thin your deck!

Try playing a few rounds to see how it handles the rules! After you've confirmed it works well, we can move on to parsing the JPEGs and setting up Pygame CE.

## Agent (2026-08-29 16:57:24)

Ah, good catch! I see exactly what happened in your test play. 

You drew cards indefinitely and managed to pull all 7 normal aging cards (`Scared 0` through `Scared 6`). However, the official rules state that **if you run out of Robinson cards in your draw pile during a fight, you suffer a penalty of 1 Life point** (in addition to adding an aging card and shuffling the discard pile to form the new draw pile).

Because I forgot to deduct the 1 Life point when the deck reshuffles, your game allowed you to loop through the deck infinitely without a penalty until you eventually bled out via the standard "pay 1 life to draw 1 card" cost. The reason you didn't see the 3 "Hard Aging" cards (`Very Stupid`) is simply because you died from drawing costs before you cycled the deck enough times to pull them!

I have updated the engine's `_shuffle_robinson_deck` method:
```python
    def _shuffle_robinson_deck(self):
        """Shuffle discard pile into draw pile, adding an aging card."""
        aging_card = self.aging_normal_deck.draw()
        if not aging_card:
            aging_card = self.aging_hard_deck.draw()

        if aging_card:
            self.robinson_discard.add(aging_card)

        self.robinson_deck.cards.extend(self.robinson_discard.cards)
        self.robinson_discard.cards.clear()
        
        # When creating a new draw pile from an empty deck in combat, 
        # we suffer 1 life penalty according to the rules!
        if self.state == GameState.FIGHTING:
            self.life -= 1
            
        self.robinson_deck.shuffle()
```
I also added a check immediately after the shuffle to immediately kill the player (`GAME_OVER_LOSS`) if this specific penalty pushes their life below 0. 

I've re-run the `pytest` suite and all tests continue to pass correctly. If you `uv run play.py` again, you will now properly bleed out much faster if you try to cycle your deck excessively!
